扩散、流与生成建模Diffusion, Flow, and Generative Modeling

228 篇228 papers
平稳Steady本周This week399 · 89.6‰上周Last week557 · 92.29‰变化Change×0.97

该主题通过扩散和流匹配推进生成建模，涵盖语言、视频、音频和科学领域。它包括少步采样、概率流 ODE，以及文本到音视频和反问题等应用。This theme advances generative modeling with diffusion and flow matching, covering language, video, audio, and scientific domains. It includes few-step sampling, probability-flow ODEs, and applications such as text-to-audio-video and inverse problems.

强化学习与后训练Reinforcement Learning and Post-Training

289 篇289 papers
平稳Steady本周This week395 · 88.7‰上周Last week659 · 109.2‰变化Change×0.81

论文通过策略蒸馏、优势塑造和 KL 正则化更新改进 LLM 推理和智能体任务的策略优化，同时进行理论收敛性和样本复杂度分析。它们还研究 RL 中的测试时训练和熵坍缩。Papers improve policy optimization for LLM reasoning and agentic tasks through on-policy distillation, advantage shaping, and KL-regularized updates, alongside theoretical convergence and sample-complexity analyses. They also study test-time training and entropy collapse in RL.

LLM 智能体与评估LLM Agents and Evaluation

323 篇323 papers
平稳Steady本周This week245 · 55.02‰上周Last week332 · 55.01‰变化Change×1.0

该主题涵盖用于编码、计算机使用和多智能体协作的基于 LLM 的智能体，以及记忆和自我改进。它还审查评估流程和 LLM 评判器，并研究人机交互和社会仿真。This theme covers LLM-based agents for coding, computer use, and multi-agent collaboration, alongside memory and self-improvement. It also audits evaluation pipelines and LLM judges, and studies human-AI interaction and social simulation.

关键词Key terms LLM judges multi-agent coding agents Agent Evaluation

多模态与语音理解Multimodal and Speech Understanding

70 篇70 papers
平稳Steady本周This week159 · 35.71‰上周Last week247 · 40.93‰变化Change×0.87

这些论文推进视觉-语言和语音模型，解决幻觉、注意力坍缩和跨模态解耦。它们还使语音系统适应领域偏移、口音和情感控制。These papers advance vision-language and speech models, tackling hallucination, attention collapse, and cross-modal disentanglement. They also adapt speech systems for domain shifts, accents, and emotion control.

科学和医学中的 AIAI for Science and Medicine

392 篇392 papers
平稳Steady本周This week91 · 20.44‰上周Last week110 · 18.23‰变化Change×1.12

这些论文将机器学习应用于科学和医学领域，从用于物理仿真的神经算子到用于医学成像和诊断的基础模型。它们还构建用于科学发现的智能体，并解决缺失模态和治疗效果估计等特定领域挑战。These papers apply machine learning to scientific and medical domains, from neural operators for physical simulation to foundation models for medical imaging and diagnosis. They also build agents for scientific discovery and address domain-specific challenges like missing modalities and treatment effect estimation.

关键词Key terms neural operators medical imaging physics-informed pathology foundation models

图与结构化数据建模Graph and Structured Data Modeling

137 篇137 papers
平稳Steady本周This week80 · 17.97‰上周Last week98 · 16.24‰变化Change×1.11

这些论文开发用于图、表格数据、时间序列和结构化检索的模型，包括图神经网络、表格基础模型和 GraphRAG。它们解决预测、上下文学习、异常检测和知识图谱推理。These papers develop models for graphs, tabular data, time series, and structured retrieval, including graph neural networks, tabular foundation models, and GraphRAG. They address forecasting, in-context learning, anomaly detection, and knowledge-graph reasoning.

AI 安全、对齐与隐私AI Safety, Alignment, and Privacy

405 篇405 papers
平稳Steady本周This week79 · 17.74‰上周Last week92 · 15.24‰变化Change×1.16

这些论文研究语言模型和智能体的漏洞与防御，包括越狱、提示注入、隐私攻击和拒绝失败。它们还开发遗忘、水印、护栏和鲁棒对齐方法。These papers examine vulnerabilities and defenses for language models and agents, including jailbreaks, prompt injection, privacy attacks, and refusal failures. They also develop unlearning, watermarking, guardrails, and robust alignment methods.

机制可解释性与引导Mechanistic Interpretability and Steering

94 篇94 papers
上升Rising本周This week45 · 10.11‰上周Last week34 · 5.63‰变化Change×1.8

这些论文剖析 transformer 中的内部表示和回路，使用稀疏自编码器、激活修补和引导来理解和控制模型行为。它们涵盖语言、视觉以及秩坍缩和诱导头等结构特性。These papers dissect internal representations and circuits in transformers, using sparse autoencoders, activation patching, and steering to understand and control model behavior. They span language, vision, and structural properties such as rank collapse and induction heads.

关键词Key terms sparse autoencoders activation steering circuit discovery induction heads

Rethinking Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation: From Alignment Coverage to Supervision Reliability

Bingxi Hou, Guochao Jiang, Guofeng Quan et al. · Hugging Face 167 赞167 Hugging Face upvotes
arXiv:2610.08448 · alphaXiv

Kandinsky 6.0 Video: Foundation Models for Synchronized Video and Audio Generation

Team Kandinsky, Julia Agafonova, Bulat Akhmatov et al. · Hugging Face 158 赞158 Hugging Face upvotes · 2026-10-06 日报推荐Highlight on 2026-10-06

关键结果Key result本文提出 Kandinsky 6.0 Video，一个用于同步文本到音视频生成的基础扩散模型系列，包括 Kandinsky 6.0 Video Lite（3B 参数）和 Kandinsky 6.0 Video Pro（29B 参数），可生成 5 秒视频片段，带有同步的 44 kHz 音频和最高全高清（1920×1080）分辨率。在并排人工评估中，Kandinsky 6.0 Video Pro 明显优于其前代 Kandinsky 5.0 Video Pro，并与领先的音视频生成模型保持竞争力，尤其在语音质量方面。The paper presents Kandinsky 6.0 Video, a family of foundation diffusion models for synchronized text-to-audio-video generation, comprising Kandinsky 6.0 Video Lite (3B parameters) and Kandinsky 6.0 Video Pro (29B parameters), which generate 5-second video clips with synchronized 44 kHz audio and up to Full-HD (1920×1080) resolution. In side-by-side human evaluation, Kandinsky 6.0 Video Pro clearly outperforms its predecessor, Kandinsky 5.0 Video Pro, and remains competitive with leading audio-video generation models, particularly in speech quality.

呈现了一个大规模同步音视频基础模型，具有出色的人工评估结果，对多模态生成研究人员很有用。Presents a large synchronized audio-video foundation model with strong human evaluation results, useful for multimodal generation researchers.

原文引文（经程序核对）Quoted from the paper (checked by program)
We present Kandinsky 6.0 Video, a family of foundation diffusion models for synchronized text-to-audio-video generation, comprising Kandinsky 6.0 Video Lite (3B parameters) and Kandinsky 6.0 Video Pro (29B parameters).
Both models generate 5-second video clips with synchronized 44 kHz audio, including lip-sync, in text-to-audio-video (T2AV) and image-to-audio-video (I2AV) modes; a built-in super-resolution model raises the output resolution to Full-HD (1920×1080).
In side-by-side human evaluation, Kandinsky 6.0 Video Pro clearly outperforms its predecessor, Kandinsky 5.0 Video Pro, and remains competitive with leading audio-video generation models, particularly in speech quality.
arXiv:2610.05608 · alphaXiv

LoGRA: Scaling LLM Reinforcement Learning with Low-Rank Gradient Sketches

Shaokun Zhang, Yifan Zhang, Jian Hu et al. · Hugging Face 120 赞120 Hugging Face upvotes · 2026-10-06 日报推荐Highlight on 2026-10-06

关键结果Key resultWith all techniques combined, LoGRA reduces average training memory usage by up to 45.7% across reasoning tasks without compromising performance, and it enables stable training of a 27B-parameter model for over 1,100 steps on a single eight-GPU node where dense Adam runs out of memory.

提出一种内存高效的 RL 后训练方法，最多将内存降低 45.7%，并能在单个 8-GPU 节点上训练 27B 参数模型，解决了重大实际瓶颈。Introduces a memory-efficient RL post-training method that reduces memory by up to 45.7% and enables 27B-parameter training on a single 8-GPU node, addressing a major practical bottleneck.

原文引文（经程序核对）Quoted from the paper (checked by program)
With all techniques combined, LoGRA reduces average training memory usage by up to 45.7% across reasoning tasks without compromising performance. It also enables stable training of a 27B-parameter model for over 1,100 steps on a single eight-GPU node, where dense Adam runs out of memory, making previously memory-infeasible RL training practical.
Over the complete runs, average memory decreases from 9.18 to 7.18 GiB at 1.5B and from 31.82 to 17.29 GiB at 7B, reductions of 21.8% and 45.7%, respectively.

RSR expands 2,001 successful source trajectories into 11,094 rewritten trajectories for supervised finetuning. Training on these trajectories outperforms both the base model and direct trajectory SFT.

Long-WAM: Scaling the Context of World-Action Models

Wei Huang, Bohan Zhang, Chenzhi Liu et al. · Hugging Face 90 赞90 Hugging Face upvotes · 2026-10-08 日报推荐Highlight on 2026-10-08

关键结果Key result我们的核心发现是，访问历史并不等同于利用历史：当视频基础模型以自回归方式（AR）预训练时，更长的历史带来的益处要大得多。在 RoboCasa GR-1 上，将上下文从 0.0 秒增加到 19.2 秒，成功率从 63.3% 提高到 78.7%，而双向预训练的初始化没有净收益；机器人领域的 AR 预训练进一步提高了 GR-1 和 LIBERO-Long 上的峰值成功率；Long-WAM 还在 LIBERO-Long、RoboTwin 2.0 和 DOMINO 上取得了对比方法中的最佳结果，现实世界部署包括动态杯子堆叠任务上 95% 的成功率。Our central finding is that access to history is not the same as using it: longer histories pay off far more when the video foundation is pretrained autoregressively (AR). On RoboCasa GR-1, increasing context from 0.0 to 19.2 seconds raises success from 63.3% to 78.7%, whereas a bidirectionally pretrained initialization shows no net gain; robot-domain AR pretraining further raises peak success on GR-1 and LIBERO-Long; Long-WAM also achieves the best results among compared methods on LIBERO-Long, RoboTwin 2.0, and DOMINO, and real-world deployment includes 95% success on dynamic cup stacking.

强有力的结果表明，自回归预训练解锁了长上下文世界-动作模型，推动了实时机器人控制的发展。Strong result showing autoregressive pretraining unlocks long-context world-action models, advancing real-time robot control.

原文引文（经程序核对）Quoted from the paper (checked by program)
Our central finding is that access to history is not the same as using it: longer histories pay off far more when the video foundation is pretrained autoregressively (AR). We first learn causal prediction from robot and egocentric videos without action labels, then preserve this history-to-future structure during world-action adaptation. On RoboCasa GR-1, increasing context from 0.0 to 19.2 seconds raises success from 63.3% to 78.7%, whereas a bidirectionally pretrained initialization shows no net gain; robot-domain AR pretraining further raises peak success on GR-1 and LIBERO-Long.
Long-WAM also achieves the best results among compared methods on LIBERO-Long, RoboTwin 2.0, and DOMINO.
Real-time deployment on Unitree G1 and YAM supports dynamic and long-horizon manipulation, including 95% success on dynamic cup stacking, where _0.5 and Fast-WAM succeed in none of 20 trials.

TRACE: Rollout-Guided Quantization-Aware Training for FP4 Reinforcement Learning of MoE Language Models

Xin Wang, Hao Yu, Zhengyang Zhuge et al. · Hugging Face 88 赞88 Hugging Face upvotes · 2026-10-07 日报推荐Highlight on 2026-10-07

关键结果Key result我们在四个大规模 MoE 语言模型上评估了 TRACE，覆盖推理、编码和长程 RL 任务。我们的结果表明，TRACE 能够实现联合 FP4 权重/激活和 FP4 KV-cache rollout，其 RL 性能与 BF16 rollout 相当，同时相比对 BF16 训练策略进行事后 FP4 量化，实现了高达 5.4 的 rollout 加速和强大的最终 FP4 性能。We evaluate TRACE on four large-scale MoE language models across reasoning, coding, and long-horizon RL tasks. Our results demonstrate that TRACE enables joint FP4 weight/activation and FP4 KV-cache rollout with RL performance comparable to BF16 rollout, while achieving up to 5.4 rollout speedup and strong final FP4 performance compared with post-hoc FP4 quantization of BF16-trained policies.

TRACE 对 MoE 模型的 FP4 RL 使用 rollout 引导的量化，在获得实际效率提升的同时直接减少了训练与 rollout 之间的差异。TRACE uses rollout-guided quantization for FP4 RL of MoE models, directly reducing train-rollout discrepancy with practical efficiency gains.

原文引文（经程序核对）Quoted from the paper (checked by program)
We evaluate TRACE on four large-scale MoE language models across reasoning, coding, and long-horizon RL tasks. Our results demonstrate that TRACE enables joint FP4 weight/activation and FP4 KV-cache rollout with RL performance comparable to BF16 rollout, while achieving up to 5.4 rollout speedup and strong final FP4 performance compared with post-hoc FP4 quantization of BF16-trained policies.

nanoMuse is a minimal, complete personal agent under the GPL-3.0: an Android app, an iOS app (TestFlight), a desktop app for macOS, Windows and Linux, a web app, and the relay, nanoMuse Cloud, with its console. The first release was on September 25, 2026; this report describes the code as of version 0.1.40 (October 2026).

Pollo AI turns creative ideas into campaigns with OpenAI

Pollo AI 服务超过 26 million 用户，推出了 Pollo Agent，这是一个基于 OpenAI 模型（包括 GPT-6 Astra 和 GPT-Image-2.5）构建的引导式视频创建工作流，使用 GPT-5.6 进行路由；30% 的创作者会话从模板或热门格式工作流开始，公司报告称用户在选择或切换模型上花费的时间减少超过 50%。其 Marketing Studio 的“Photo to Video Ads”工具将静态图像和提示转化为短视频，一位用户用它制作了大约 15 秒的香水广告。Pollo AI, which serves more than 26 million users, launched Pollo Agent, a guided video-creation workflow built on OpenAI models including GPT-6 Astra and GPT-Image-2.5, with GPT-5.6 used for routing; 30% of creator sessions start from templates or trending-format workflows, and the company reports users spend more than 50% less time choosing or switching models. Its Marketing Studio Photo to Video Ads tool turns a still image and prompt into a short video, which one user used to produce a roughly 15-second fragrance ad.

OpenAI · 2026-10-08

LegalOn halves Codex costs while maintaining development speed

LegalOn Technologies 将 OpenAI 的 Codex 集成到其开发流程中，通过将 GPT-6 Luna、GPT-6.1 Sol 和 GPT-6 Astra 与任务复杂度匹配，并应用部门和个人的月度用量限制，报告称每日估算成本降低 65%，成熟业务领域成本降低 20%，同时保持开发速度。该公司最初让开发人员无限使用 GPT-5.5 的 Fast 模式，随后默认限制 Fast 模式并使用并行任务执行。LegalOn Technologies integrated OpenAI's Codex into its development process and, by matching GPT-6 Luna, GPT-6.1 Sol, and GPT-6 Astra to task complexity and applying department- and individual-level monthly usage limits, reports a 65% reduction in estimated daily costs and a 20% cost reduction in mature business areas while maintaining development speed. The company had initially given developers unlimited access to GPT-5.5 in Fast mode, then restricted Fast mode by default and used parallel task execution.

OpenAI · 2026-10-08

Disrupting AI-enabled “false front” operations

OpenAI 报告称已封禁两个隐蔽影响行动，一个来自俄罗斯，一个来自伊朗，它们将其模型与传统技术结合，为“虚假前台”实体提供支持；伊朗行动运营了七个“记者”人格，推销长篇报道，而俄罗斯行动则利用不知情的拉丁美洲人员运营一个“智库”。OpenAI 将源自俄罗斯的行动评估为 IO Breakout Scale 上的 Category 5，这是其开始报告以来首次 Category 5 级别的破坏；将源自伊朗的行动评估为 Category 4。在过去两年半中，已揭露 30 个隐蔽影响行动。OpenAI reported banning two covert influence operations, one from Russia and one from Iran, that combined its models with traditional techniques to support “false front” entities; the Iranian operation ran seven “journalist” personas pitching long-form articles, while the Russian operation co-opted unwitting people in Latin America to run a “think tank.” OpenAI assessed the Russia-origin operation as Category 5 on the IO Breakout Scale—its first Category 5 disruption since it began reporting—and the Iran-origin operation as Category 4, among 30 covert influence operations exposed in the last two and a half years.

Google DeepMind · 2026-10-06

EmbeddingGemma 2: an open, lightweight multimodal embedding model

Google DeepMind 推出了 EmbeddingGemma 2，这是一个基于 Gemma 4 的 Apache 2.0 许可多模态嵌入模型，拥有 740 million 参数，将文本、图像、音频和视频映射到统一的嵌入空间。对于纯文本工作负载，它仅需 270M 参数，可选 170M 视觉编码器和 300M 音频编码器，支持高达 8K token 上下文，并将 MTEB Code 从 68.76 提高到 78.68（提升 9.92 分）；在 Google Pixel 11 Pro 上，纯文本权重的设备内存低至约 191MB，完整多模态模型约 567MB。Google DeepMind launched EmbeddingGemma 2, an Apache 2.0 licensed multimodal embedding model built on Gemma 4 with 740 million parameters, mapping text, images, audio, and video into a unified embedding space. It uses as little as 270M parameters for text-only workloads with optional 170M vision and 300M audio encoders, supports up to an 8K token context, and improves MTEB Code from 68.76 to 78.68 (a 9.92-point gain), with on-device memory as low as ~191MB for text-only weights and ~567MB for the full multimodal model on a Google Pixel 11 Pro.

Google Research · 2026-10-07

Does better work always mean better workers?

一项为期三个月、涉及 11 家知识产权律师事务所 133 名专利律师的随机对照试验发现，使用 AI 使专利撰写得分在 10 天时提高了 0.34 个标准差（提升 10 个百分位点），在 90 天时提高了 0.38 SD（提升 11 个百分位点），增益来自更少的差评分和更多的好评分，而不是更多的优秀评分。对学习的影响因资历而异：使用 AI 90 天的资深律师表现出更强的判断力，而初级律师平均技能没有提高，得分分化为更多的高分和更多的低分。A three-month randomized controlled trial with 133 patent attorneys at eleven IP law firms found that AI access raised patent-drafting scores by 0.34 standard deviations at 10 days (a 10-percentile-point gain) and 0.38 SD at 90 days (11 percentile points), with gains coming from fewer poor scores and more good scores rather than more excellent ones. Effects on learning differed by seniority: senior lawyers who used AI for 90 days showed stronger judgment, while junior lawyers showed no average skill gain, with scores splitting into more strong and more low scores.

Google Research · 2026-10-06

Unlocking Earth AI’s planetary geospatial foundation models for global public health

Google Research 发布了 Population Dynamics Foundation Model (PDFM)，它是 Google Earth AI 的一部分，将聚合搜索趋势、建成环境和流动性信号以及环境决定因素压缩为每月更新的位置嵌入。文章报告了五个由合作伙伴推动的案例研究，在这些研究中，现成的 PDFM 位置嵌入在疾病领域、地理环境和流行病学任务中与常规输入相当或有所改进，无需针对特定任务微调。Google Research presents the Population Dynamics Foundation Model (PDFM), part of Google Earth AI, which compresses aggregated search trends, built-environment and mobility signals, and environmental determinants into location embeddings refreshed at a monthly cadence. The post reports five partner-driven case studies in which off-the-shelf PDFM location embeddings matched or improved on conventional inputs across disease domains, geographic settings, and epidemiological tasks without task-specific fine-tuning.

Google Research · 2026-10-05

Open and Emergent Problems in Agentic Privacy and Security: A Contextual Angle

Google Research 发布了一份研讨会报告《Open and Emergent Problems in Agentic Privacy and Security: A Contextual Angle》，基于 2025 年底在纽约市举行的 Google CAPS Workshop，该研讨会汇集了 50 多位学术界和产业界领袖。报告运用 Contextual Integrity 理论，从系统、模型、用户和生态系统层面阐述智能体隐私与安全挑战，并将其从信息共享扩展到智能体行为的适当性。Google Research published a workshop report, "Open and Emergent Problems in Agentic Privacy and Security: A Contextual Angle," drawing on a Google CAPS Workshop held in late 2025 in New York City that brought together more than 50 academic and industry leaders. The report frames agent privacy and security challenges at system, model, user, and ecosystem levels using the theory of Contextual Integrity, extended from information sharing to the appropriateness of agent actions.

Google AI · 2026-10-07

Introducing Playground: Create and play custom games

Google 推出了 Playground，这是一个基于浏览器的实验性平台，用户通过文本提示描述即可创建、游玩和分享自定义游戏，无需编写代码。游戏可以保持私密、通过链接分享，或经过安全审核后发布到社区画廊；它支持排行榜和特定类型的多人游戏，并计划与 Unity Spark 集成以支持更高级的机制和 3D 能力。Google introduced Playground, an experimental browser-based platform where users create, play, and share custom games by describing them in text prompts, with no coding required. Games can be kept private, shared by link, or published to a community gallery with safety screenings, and it supports leaderboards and multiplayer in select genres; an integration with Unity Spark for more advanced mechanics and 3D capabilities is planned.

Anthropic · 2026-10-08

2026 Usage Policy update

Anthropic 发布了新的使用政策，将于 November 12 生效，将散落在选举、欺诈、隐私和虚假信息章节中的欺骗性活动规则整合到新章节“Do Not Engage in Deceptive Campaigns or Artificial Activity”中，并将选举章节更名为“Do Not Undermine Democratic Processes”。该更新还取消了对个性化投票和竞选定位的全面禁止，澄清武器禁令涵盖制导与控制软件以及武装无人机，并重写了监控与刑事司法章节。Anthropic published a new Usage Policy taking effect November 12 that consolidates deceptive-activity rules scattered across elections, fraud, privacy, and disinformation sections into a new section titled 'Do Not Engage in Deceptive Campaigns or Artificial Activity,' and renames the elections section 'Do Not Undermine Democratic Processes.' The update also removes the blanket prohibition on personalized vote and campaign targeting, clarifies that weapons prohibitions cover guidance and control software and arming drones, and rewrites the surveillance and criminal justice section.

Over those three years it will provide Claude, Claude Code, and API credits to several hundred Genesis Mission research projects, partner with agencies and national laboratories on priorities including fusion energy and quantum computing, and provide training and technical support.

Introducing Claude Haiku 5.5

It is the first Haiku-class model with an adjustable effort setting; Anthropic also halved the price of Claude Sonnet 5.5 cache reads (about 20% cheaper on most agentic work) and introduced a monthly API credit for Claude Max and Team subscribers.

Microsoft Research · 2026-10-07

Agent Lightning v1.0: A 3,500-Line Lightweight Agentic RL Framework for Training Agents with Real Harnesses

微软亚洲研究院发布了 Agent Lightning v1.0，这是一个约 3,500 行代码的 Harnessed Agentic RL 框架，通过 LLM 代理使用真实部署 harness 训练智能体，原生支持 Kubernetes，且不依赖付费商业沙盒服务。端到端编码智能体流程使用约 6,000 个训练样本，将 Qwen3.5-9B 在 SWE-bench Verified 上的 Pass@1 从 41.8% 提高到 56.4%（提升 14.6 个百分点）。Microsoft Research Asia released Agent Lightning v1.0, a Harnessed Agentic RL framework of roughly 3,500 lines of code that trains agents using their real deployment harness via an LLM proxy, with native Kubernetes support and no dependency on paid commercial sandbox services. An end-to-end coding agent pipeline raised Qwen3.5-9B from 41.8% to 56.4% Pass@1 on SWE-bench Verified (a 14.6 percentage point gain) using about 6,000 training samples.

Microsoft Research · 2026-10-06

What AI gets wrong and what failure teaches us

微软研究院播客的一期节目邀请微软研究院合作研究经理 Jennifer Neville 与 Chad Atalla 对话，讨论评估在推动 AI 性能中的作用，以及模型在传统基准之外表现出的令人意外的失败。节目中提到 Neville 已发表超过 130 篇论文，引用超过 10,000 次。A Microsoft Research Podcast episode features Jennifer Neville, a partner research manager at Microsoft Research, in conversation with Chad Atalla about evaluation's role in pushing AI performance and the surprising failures models show beyond traditional benchmarks. The episode notes Neville has published more than 130 papers with over 10,000 citations.

NVIDIA · 2026-10-08

Into the Omniverse: How Developers Turn Ideas Into Simulations With Frontier AI Agents

NVIDIA 介绍了多个项目，开发人员通过自然语言指令指导 GPT-6 Astra 等前沿模型与 Omniverse 库配合工作，其中一位 Omniverse 产品经理构建了仓库人形机器人仿真器，一位仿真技术经理构建了 Zero to Alpamayo——一个基于旧金山 Market Street 的可复用环境，并有一个独立的 Cosmos3-Nano 实验用于变化天气和照明。另一个项目中，一位 RTX 传感器验证工程师指导 Astra 和 Claude Fable 5 智能体将 ovrtx 相机和原始 LiDAR 输出与记录数据进行比较，在大约三天内从零创建了两个数字孪生，并改进了两个现有模型。NVIDIA described projects in which developers direct frontier models such as GPT-6 Astra through natural-language instructions to work with Omniverse libraries, including an Omniverse product manager building a warehouse humanoid simulator and a simulation technology manager building Zero to Alpamayo, a reusable environment based on San Francisco's Market Street, with a separate Cosmos3-Nano experiment varying weather and lighting. In another project, an RTX sensor validation engineer directed Astra and Claude Fable 5 agents to compare ovrtx camera and raw LiDAR outputs with recorded data, creating two digital twins from scratch and improving two existing ones over about three days.

Why Telecom Operators Are Building Their AI Strategy on Open Models

NVIDIA Developer · 2026-10-08

5 Steps to Create SimReady Assets for Robotics with Frontier AI Models

NVIDIA 概述了一个五步 Omniverse 和 SimReady Foundation 工作流，用于将 ABB YuMi 机器人从 STEP 文件转换为仿真就绪的 OpenUSD 资产，GPT-6 Astra 等前沿模型编写调用 Omniverse 工具的 Python 代码。物理配置使用制造商数据表和公开 URDF，接触摩擦系数为静摩擦 0.8、动摩擦 0.6；双臂和夹具完成了四个取放循环，使用彩色方块和根据参考图像建模的 Sharpie 马克笔。NVIDIA outlined a five-step Omniverse and SimReady Foundation workflow for converting an ABB YuMi robot from STEP files into a simulation-ready OpenUSD asset, with frontier models such as GPT-6 Astra writing Python code that calls Omniverse tools. Physics configuration used manufacturer datasheets and a public URDF, with contact friction coefficients of 0.8 static and 0.6 dynamic, and both arms and grippers completed four pick-and-place cycles with colored cubes and a Sharpie marker modeled from a reference image.

NVIDIA Developer · 2026-10-08

Building Reliable Data Analytics Agents: Lessons from the KDD Cup

NVIDIA 的 KGMON 团队在 KDD Cup 2026 Data Agents 竞赛中获得第二名，他们围绕一个小型固定 LLM 构建了受限 harness，将 CSV 和 JSON 文件转换为一个 SQLite 数据库中的表，并仅暴露有限的 schema() 和 sql() 接口。该智能体仅限于 schema()、sql(query)、write_answer(df) 和 prose_helper()，并通过预检 schema 侦察步骤和执行轨迹，由专门的 inspector 智能体对失败进行分类。NVIDIA's KGMON team placed second in the KDD Cup 2026 Data Agents competition with a constrained harness around a small, fixed LLM, converting CSV and JSON files into tables in one SQLite database behind narrow schema() and sql() interfaces. The agent was limited to schema(), sql(query), write_answer(df), and prose_helper(), with a preflight schema-scouting step and execution traces used by a specialized inspector agent to categorize failures.

NVIDIA Developer · 2026-10-07

The Machines that Make the Machines

NVIDIA 西雅图机器人实验室和 Isaac 工程团队开发了用于组装 GB300 测试托盘的机器人系统，目标包括母线组装和多连接器插入。使用 FoundationPose、Lissajous 曲线路径点基元和阻抗控制器的经典模块化流水线在母线组装上实现了超过 95% 的成功率，循环时间为 160 秒；连接器抓取使用了一种名为 DOPER 的新位姿估计框架，插入策略在 Isaac Lab 中通过 sim-to-real RL 预训练，然后通过 SPARR 残差训练结合力矩输入进行精细调整。NVIDIA's Seattle Robotics Lab and Isaac engineering team developed robotic systems to assemble GB300 tester trays, targeting busbar assembly and multi-connector insertion. A classical modular pipeline using FoundationPose, Lissajous-curve waypoint primitives, and an impedance controller achieved over 95% success on busbar assembly with a 160-second cycle time; connector grasping used a new pose-estimation framework called DOPER, and insertion policies were pretrained in Isaac Lab with sim-to-real RL then refined via SPARR residual training with force-torque inputs.

NVIDIA Developer · 2026-10-07

Validate AI Factory Changes with Digital Twins and AI Agents

NVIDIA 介绍了使用 NVIDIA DSX Air（AI 工厂基础设施和软件接口的基于节点的数字孪生）与 NVIDIA Brev 按需 GPU 计算相结合，在硬件到位之前验证配置和软件变更。智能体工作流查询该数字孪生、运行配置检查、将结果与策略进行比较，并在受治理的循环内生成有据可依的建议；NVIDIA AI Blueprint for Video Search and Summarization 展示了这一模式。NVIDIA described using NVIDIA DSX Air, a node-based digital twin of AI factory infrastructure and software interfaces, together with NVIDIA Brev on-demand GPU compute, to validate configurations and software changes before hardware arrives. Agentic workflows interrogate the twin, run configuration checks, compare outcomes against policy, and produce evidence-backed recommendations within a governed loop, with the NVIDIA AI Blueprint for Video Search and Summarization demonstrating the pattern.

Hugging Face · 2026-10-08

The model that didn't exist, so you made it yourself

Hugging Face 报告称，在发现官方 Qwen-Image 2.1 prompt rewriter 的 9B 模型只有压缩副本且需要约 20 GB 内存后，使用 ML Intern 创建了 0.8B 版本；该 0.8B 模型可在 CPU 上运行，99.7% 的时间返回有效输出，并且使用的 token 约为教师模型的四分之一。项目成本为 USD 16，包括使用 9B 模型标注 8,797 条示例请求；作者还用相同方法制作了另外五个模型。Hugging Face reported using ML Intern to create a 0.8B version of the Qwen-Image 2.1 prompt rewriter after finding only compressed copies of the official 9B model that needs about 20 GB of memory; the 0.8B model runs on a CPU, returns valid output 99.7% of the time, and uses about a quarter of the teacher's tokens. The project cost USD 16, including labeling 8,797 example requests with the 9B model, and the author made five more models the same way.

Hugging Face · 2026-10-07

Multimodal open d1 decision models for the edge

Hugging Face 发布了两个开放决策模型 d1-3B 和 d1-omni-600M（实验性），它们构建于 Liquid Foundation Models 之上，通过单次前向传播返回答案，而不是生成 token。d1-3B 在 Decision Index 0.2.1 上得分为 48.57，在 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor 上回答问题耗时 16 ms，在 Jetson AGX Orin 上为 26 ms，在 Orin Nano 上为 50 ms，在七个数据集上的平均得分为 82.9；d1-omni-600M 得分为 78.4，支持文本与图像或音频。Hugging Face released two open decision models, d1-3B and d1-omni-600M (experimental), built on Liquid Foundation Models and answering in a single forward pass rather than generating tokens. d1-3B scores 48.57 on the Decision Index 0.2.1 and answers a question in 16 ms on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor, 26 ms on a Jetson AGX Orin, and 50 ms on an Orin Nano, with a mean score of 82.9 across seven datasets; d1-omni-600M scores 78.4 and supports text with images or audio.

Hugging Face · 2026-10-07

Introducing Falcon ASR

技术创新研究所推出了 Falcon-ASR，这是一个用于阿拉伯语的 1.6 billion 参数语音识别模型，重点关注阿联酋方言，同时使用相同权重支持英语、法语、西班牙语和葡萄牙语。它在六个阿拉伯语测试集上取得 20.92% 的平均 WER，而排行榜快照中最佳结果为 23.17%；在内部阿联酋语评估中 WER 为 22.73%，CER 为 10.19%；在七个公开英语测试集上平均 WER 为 5.74%。The Technology Innovation Institute introduced Falcon-ASR, a 1.6 billion parameter speech recognition model for Arabic with a focus on the Emirati dialect, also supporting English, French, Spanish, and Portuguese with the same weights. It achieved 20.92% average WER across six Arabic test sets versus the best published 23.17% in the leaderboard snapshot, 22.73% WER and 10.19% CER on an internal Emirati evaluation, and 5.74% mean WER on seven public English test sets.

Hugging Face · 2026-10-07

One Model Family, Two Gold-Level Results: Fine-Tuning Nemotron for IOI and IMO

Hugging Face 报告称，经过微调的 Nemotron 模型在 IMO 2026 和 IOI 2026 上均达到金牌水平：Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC 结合 SFT 和 GenCorrect 在 IOI 上获得 535.4/600 分，高于 361.12 的金牌线以及人类最高分 498.27；一个 Nemotron 3 Ultra 的 generate-verify-refine 系统在 IMO 上获得 30/42 分，高于 29 分的官方金牌线。训练使用了 22,000 个精选竞赛编程题目，以及包含 414,890 个样本、涵盖 15,818 个不同证明问题的 SFT 语料，并在 9,597 个证明问题上进行 RL。Hugging Face reported that fine-tuned Nemotron models reached gold-medal level at both IMO 2026 and IOI 2026: Nemotron-3-Ultra-CC with SFT and GenCorrect scored 535.4/600 at IOI, above the 361.12 gold threshold and the top human score of 498.27, and a Nemotron 3 Ultra generate-verify-refine system scored 30/42 at IMO, above the official gold threshold of 29. Training used 22,000 curated competitive-programming problems and an SFT corpus of 414,890 examples across 15,818 unique proof problems, with RL on 9,597 proof problems.

Apple Machine Learning Research · 2026-10-08

Normalizing Trajectory Models

苹果的研究人员提出了 Normalizing Trajectory Models (NTM)，将每个逆向步骤建模为具有精确似然训练的条件归一化流，并结合浅层可逆块与跨轨迹的深层并行预测器。NTM 的精确轨迹似然支持自蒸馏，在四步内生成高质量样本，在四个采样步骤中于文本到图像基准上达到或超越强图像生成基线。Apple researchers introduced Normalizing Trajectory Models (NTM), which model each reverse step as a conditional normalizing flow with exact likelihood training and combine shallow invertible blocks with a deep parallel predictor across the trajectory. NTM's exact trajectory likelihood enables self-distillation to produce high-quality samples in four steps, matching or outperforming strong image generation baselines on text-to-image benchmarks in four sampling steps.

Apple Machine Learning Research · 2026-10-06

RISED: Rubrics for Agentic Multi-Environment Selection and Self-Distillation

苹果的研究人员提出了 RISED，该方法在多环境智能体 RL 中重新利用评标标准进行在线数据选择和策略监督：LLM 评判器使用共享评标词汇为每个 rollout 打标签，正面评标为在策略自蒸馏教师提供特权上下文，负面评标引导后续 rollout 生成远离失败模式。在各种模型骨干上，RISED 在所有环境中取得最高平均通过率，并在每个单独环境中排名第一或第二。Apple researchers presented RISED, which repurposes rubrics for online data selection and policy supervision in multi-environment agent RL: an LLM judge tags each rollout with a shared rubric vocabulary, positive rubrics supply privileged context for an on-policy self-distillation teacher, and negative rubrics steer subsequent rollout generation away from failure modes. Across model backbones RISED achieves the highest mean pass rate across environments and ranks first or second in every individual environment.

Apple Machine Learning Research · 2026-10-05

Negotiating Ontological Boundaries in User-Authored Personal Sensing Systems

苹果关联研究人员（与斯坦福大学合作）报告了一项为期一周的探索性研究，参与者使用两个开放式 Wizard of Oz 探针之一，就他们自己定义的现象训练个性化机器学习系统。研究确定了四个本体论边界被协商的场所：现象的边界、作为关系一部分的主体、什么是信号什么是噪声，以及数据的客观性。Apple-affiliated researchers (with Stanford University) report a week-long exploratory study in which participants used one of two open-ended Wizard of Oz probes to train a personalized machine learning system on phenomena they defined themselves. The study identifies four sites where ontological boundaries were negotiated: the boundaries of a phenomenon, the subject as part of relations, what is signal and what is noise, and the objectivity of data.

AWS Machine Learning · 2026-10-08

Pay-per-inference for AI agents: How BlockRun and Incarna use Amazon Bedrock AgentCore payments

Incarna 使用 Amazon Bedrock AgentCore payments，让其智能体按每次推理请求向 BlockRun 付费，将添加 x402 支付支持的工作从数月缩短到数天，并上线端到端按推理付费流程。BlockRun 是按使用量付费的推理路由器，通过 x402 服务于来自超过 15 家提供商的超过 90 个模型；AgentCore payments 在 Base 网络上以 USDC 结算，并在基础设施层强制执行支出限制。Incarna used Amazon Bedrock AgentCore payments to let its agents pay BlockRun per inference request, cutting work to add x402 payment support from months to days and putting an end-to-end pay-per-inference flow into production. BlockRun is a pay-as-you-go inference router serving more than 90 models from more than 15 providers over x402, and AgentCore payments settles in USDC on the Base network with spending limits enforced at the infrastructure layer.

AWS Machine Learning · 2026-10-08

Share GPU clusters across teams with isolation and fairness using Amazon SageMaker HyperPod

AWS 发布了一个参考架构，用于多租户共享单个 Amazon SageMaker HyperPod EKS 集群，使用 AWS IAM Identity Center 进行集中认证，为每个团队设置 SageMaker AI 域，使用 Kubernetes 命名空间实现工作负载隔离，通过 HyperPod Task Governance 进行资源分配，并在命名空间级别进行成本分摊用于计费。示例展示了两个团队（Team A 和 Team B）在独立的命名空间中运行，拥有各自的权限集和 IAM 角色。AWS published a reference architecture for multi-tenant sharing of a single Amazon SageMaker HyperPod EKS cluster, using AWS IAM Identity Center for centralized authentication, per-team SageMaker AI domains, Kubernetes namespaces for workload isolation, HyperPod Task Governance for resource allocation, and namespace-level cost allocation for chargeback. The example shows two teams (Team A and Team B) operating in separate namespaces with per-team permission sets and IAM roles.

AWS Machine Learning · 2026-10-07

Introducing Claude Haiku 5.5 on AWS

AWS 宣布 Claude Haiku 5.5 在 Amazon Bedrock 和 AWS 上的 Claude Platform 可用，称其为 Claude 5.5 系列中最快、最高效的模型，适用于子智能体和高吞吐、成本敏感的工作，在大多数任务上成本比 Claude Haiku 4.5 低约 75%。它是首个具有 effort 控制功能的 Haiku 模型，AWS 将其定位为与 Claude Opus 5.5 搭配，后者处理规划和判断类任务。AWS announced availability of Claude Haiku 5.5 on Amazon Bedrock and Claude Platform on AWS, describing it as the fastest and most efficient model in the Claude 5.5 family for subagents and high-volume, cost-sensitive work, at around 75 percent less cost than Claude Haiku 4.5 for most tasks. It is the first Haiku model with effort controls, and AWS positions it as pairing with Claude Opus 5.5, which handles planning and judgment calls.

AWS Machine Learning · 2026-10-07

Rethinking access control for RAG with Amazon Quick and Amazon Bedrock

AWS 介绍了为 Amazon Quick 和 Amazon Bedrock Knowledge Bases 上的 RAG 添加实时访问控制列表（ACL）执行，在查询时直接与权威源核对权限，而不是依赖复制过来的 ACL 属性。文章指出了复制再过滤方法的三个弱点：AI 系统不是事实来源、基于拉取的同步导致权限陈旧从而产生安全漏洞，以及数据源权限模型的演进可能破坏 ACL 映射。AWS described adding real-time access control list enforcement for RAG on Amazon Quick and Amazon Bedrock Knowledge Bases, checking permissions directly with the authoritative source at query time rather than relying on replicated ACL attributes. The post identifies three weaknesses of the replicate-and-filter approach: the AI system is not the source of truth, stale permissions from pull-based syncs create security gaps, and evolving data source permission models can break ACL mapping.
